# Y2Linux + Reborn community beta feature audit

Retired 2026-10-18 (historical record). Its last entry predates the first public beta. Each public beta since has its own row in the [roadmap gap audit](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2Froadmap-gap-audit.md) and its own report in `docs/validation/`; the 1.0 readiness review is [V1-RELEASE-READINESS](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2FV1-RELEASE-READINESS.md). Nothing below is current.

## Baseline01 physical gate interrupted / USB diagnosis pending — 2026-10-04

Exact installed Baseline01 Linux `98daf3f` / Reborn `9cd12bae`, kernel baseline-01 and root v1.24 passes original machine/owner smoke on boot `ef2c8f2f…`, taint 0. All-four-core C1, 18 hotplug transitions, five OPP/voltage readbacks and natural parking/C2 pass; C2 has 4,857 entries and 85.4572% residency in 60.05534 seconds, zero restore faults. Eight scratch hash rounds each on DATA/SD after C2 pass. The owner restarted after an overlong deliberate USB-off C3 topology wait; C3 has no recorded armed/entered cycle and remains unqualified, not a proven C3 crash. Recovered boot `9d4d0245…` logs a DRM WARNING; automatic C3 correctly reports Skipped. Earlier post-flash USB disconnects remain independently open. Host ECM static-IP/autoconnect matching is corrected and saved; the host harness now keeps USB attached during parking and bounds its offline window to 180 s. Thirty affected host tests pass; installed firmware/images are unchanged. The owner prioritizes the separate wired-audio session holding the shared lock; USB diagnosis waits for its completion. No further CPU/transfer/suspend stress. Full Power and RTC wake remain separately NOT_RUN, historical Baseline02 RTC FAIL stays open, and endurance/electrical/distribution gates remain unqualified. See [the physical receipt](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-BASELINE-PHYSICAL.md). All 233 feature identities are retained; sealed package metadata remains its seal-time record. No main merge, push, flash or public/physical promotion. Wired-precision correction: [the current evidence record](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-HIGHRES-AUDIO-COMPLETION.md) withdraws the HARDWARE_PROVEN_UNSUPPORTED conclusion. MON0 defines16-bit monitor fields; an absolute silicon transport ceiling does not follow from that alone. Installed S16 and software24 preservation remain validated; wider hardware feasibility is open. No native24/32 qualification is claimed.

## Independent final baseline — 2026-10-04

Current software handoff: Y2LINUX-PLATFORM-V1-BASELINE-01 — READY_FOR_PLATFORM_V1_PHYSICAL_QUALIFICATION. Compiled Linux `98daf3f0220f4a1580bb4893094ee9f0e02362a5` / Reborn `9cd12baeeb70b204e08299bd4c91c8c35604c304`; kernel `6.18.0-y2linux-platform-v1-baseline-01`, root `2025.02.18-platform-v1.24`. Canonical owner candidate: `/home/luca/Dokumente/Code/Y2Linux/out/y2linux-platform-v1-baseline-candidate`. Fresh 475 production cases / 465 passed / ten dependency skips, all ten independently passed; 324 Reborn tests, fmt/strict Clippy/fresh kernel/ARM/QEMU/package/source/privacy PASS. SOFTWARE_VALIDATED / INTEGRATED_PHYSICAL_NOT_RUN; PUBLIC_DISTRIBUTION_NOT_READY. UART01 CPU hardware is inherited; Baseline02 RTC FAIL remains unresolved. Policy-enabled C3, native S16, battery safeguards, DATA/layout and recovery stay.

See [baseline](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-BASELINE.md), [twelve sessions](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-FINAL-SESSION-RESULTS.md), [independent integration review](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-INDEPENDENT-VERIFICATION.md) and [one smoke-gated physical campaign](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-PHYSICAL-QUALIFICATION.md). This current section supersedes candidate/build/acceptance wording in the dated master planning and execution record below. Older NOT_CREATED/NOT_BUILT/accepted-none and in-progress text describes its observation time, never the current baseline. No main merge, push, flash or physical/public milestone promotion occurred.

## Historical feature/planning snapshots (retained)

## Sealed integration current state — 2026-10-04

READY_FOR_PLATFORM_V1_PHYSICAL_QUALIFICATION. Compiled Linux `4c72466f2f27ddf9f0ca18ba8ddc4d89c52599d2` / Reborn `dd73dcc3243045cd33e319dda2f76918ed37ea31`. One candidate `/home/luca/Dokumente/Code/worktrees/platform-v1-integration/Y2Linux/out/y2linux-platform-v1-integration-candidate`. Integrated physical/endurance NOT_RUN, public distribution blocked. Later documentation commits are separate from the compiled pair. The [master](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-MASTER.md) and [boundary audit](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2Froadmap-gap-audit.md%23platform-10-software-exit--physical-qualification-preparation--2026-10-04&anchor=platform-10-software-exit--physical-qualification-preparation--2026-10-04) control current acceptance; the dated sections below remain retained progress/history.

## Platform 1.0 composition — 2026-10-04

All eleven source handoffs are composed on isolated paired master branches. State: INTEGRATION_IN_PROGRESS. One fresh integrated candidate is being prepared; integrated hardware and endurance are NOT_RUN. UART01 CPU and Baseline02/temporary-binary receipts remain source/image-bound. The later Baseline02 full RTC wake trial failed and does not qualify the new suspend fix.

The [233-feature JSON](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/docs/release/Y2-COMMUNITY-BETA-FEATURES.json) adds six current axes for every feature: implementation, software, physical, release, hardware condition and distribution condition. Its historical feature/patch/receipt/census records remain intact. The current patch ledger contains 50 exact manifest overlays; dated tables/counts below preserve their original cutoff. The [master](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-MASTER.md) and [physical plan](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-PLATFORM-V1-PHYSICAL-QUALIFICATION.md) control current gates.

F134 is split explicitly: implemented MTP needs integrated desktop acceptance; mass storage and RNDIS remain absent. Linux owns the filesystem throughout MTP; no block export is introduced. Verified durable quiet generations trigger one post-transfer library refresh and are deferred across sleep preparation.

## Retained Baseline02 summary — 2026-10-03

The newest sealed candidate is Y2LINUX-BASELINE-02. UART01 proves C1, C2 and C3 physically working; Baseline02 adds automatic qualified CPU0 C3 startup while preserving entry/fault guards. Baseline02 new-image/cold-boot hardware remains PHYSICAL_NOT_RUN. Full system suspend, endurance, electrical battery savings, native wider/high-rate audio and distribution decisions remain independent.

The 233-feature inventory and JSON below separate compiled source, enabled policy, exact-image physical evidence and release permission. Sections with dated snapshots are history; they do not override this baseline. This is a documentation-only synchronization of retained receipts.

### Evidence and maturity rules

The 233-feature target and product/distribution axes remain unchanged. Feature rows now include newer UART01 receipts where relevant; earlier snapshots are retained in the JSON historical fields. PHYSICALLY_QUALIFIED denotes the cited bounded exact-image behavior, not current Baseline02 acceptance. Every `current_candidate_physically_qualified` flag is false for the uninstalled Baseline02. No ENDURANCE_QUALIFIED result or electrical battery gain is invented. C3 hardware support and bounded reset/restore are proven; new automatic cold boot, wider wake sources and full system suspend have separate gates.

The 2026-10-02 lexical file/marker/gate censuses and repository histories are dated snapshots, not a fresh scan of Baseline02. The current patch ledger follows all 49 active overlays. Source implementation and physical evidence are independent of release permission and the A–G completion groups. C3 leaves implementation group A; it retains affected exact-image acceptance in B and its original hardware-conditional product classification in C.

## 4. KERNEL / DRIVERS

Production is Linux 6.18, ARMv7 little-endian Cortex-A7, EABI hard-float userspace, four SMP CPUs, HZ100, high-resolution timers/NO_HZ_IDLE, NEON/VFP, HIGHMEM and standard proc/sysfs/evdev/ALSA/DRM interfaces. LPAE, virtualization, swap, CMA, kexec/crash dump, module unloading, generic DMA engines, GPIO userspace and `/dev/mem` are disabled. Driver-managed DMA remains used by individual peripherals. `CONFIG_SECURITY=n` and `CONFIG_RANDOMIZE_BASE=n` must not be described as a hardened sandbox.

The production DTS includes the main board DTS. Memory begins at `0x80000000`, length `0x3e000000`; the loader reservation is `0x81800000 + 0x02800000`, and high/radio reservation `0xbdf00000 + 0x02100000`. Physical01 reported 952288 KiB MemTotal. Neither the advertised RAM window nor measured free RAM licenses changing reservations. BOOTIMG owns modules, carried into `/run/y2/modules` then bind-mounted into root. Preserve the existing boot budgets and mapping. Sources: [production config](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/config/production.config), [board DT](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/dts/innioasis-y2.dts), [production DT](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/dts/innioasis-y2-production.dts), [init](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/initramfs/production/init).

Project platform code also supplies clocks, pinctrl/EINT, PWRAP write policy, MT6323 ADC/charger, thermal, SPM/CIRQ, retained PM journal, USB PHY/MUSB, APT32F wheel, keypad, panel/backlight, MFG clock/domain, audio machine/AFE and native CONSYS/BTIF/Wi-Fi adaptation. The vendor Wi-Fi subtree is substantial; its unused AP/P2P/BOW/WAPI paths and TODOs are not automatically supported features. No current speaker/AW87559 or FM product path was found; historical stock bindings are not implementation evidence.

### Every active Y2 kernel patch

All 49 `.patch` files are present in the active overlay manifest; there are no orphan patches among these files. Missing numbers0040–0051 are not evidence of missing functionality. Build integration also copies platform source, so patch count alone is not driver coverage. Risk below means the cost of changing it; evidence is deliberately scoped. No patch is proved obsolete by this audit. In particular0009's name is obsolete, its implementation is
