## 5. CPU / POWER

### Current CPU idle evidence — UART01 carried into Baseline02

C1 WORKING; C2 WORKING; C3 WORKING_AND_REPEATEDLY_OBSERVED on exact UART01. C1 advances on all four cores with zero rejections and 400 bounded wakes. C2 adds 6,971 entries / 50.654676 s in 60.054881 s (84.3473%); restore failures are zero. Natural parking owns mask 0xe and reaches CPU0 with both secondary power copies zero. Eighteen supported hotplug transitions and demand restore pass.

C3 completes 21 budget-one guarded reset-and-return trials with retained SRAM proof and RGU backstop, then adds 3,624 entries / 39.204662 s in 60.088344 s (65.2450%) under normal policy. Final entry/resume/success/UART-ACK accounting is 3,647 each, same boot, taint 0, with zero timer/context/CIRQ/clock restore failures. CPU utilization is 10.28%, IRQ 163.36/s and context switches 332.04/s; these are runtime indicators, not battery-current measurements.

Baseline02 retains the physically qualified entry/restore implementation. Its new `S05y2-cpu-idle` startup checks static foundations, admits budget -1 / CPU0 state2 with readback and rolls back on failure. CPU1–3 C3 remains disabled. Stop via `y2-platform cpu-idle-policy stop`; recovery options `y2.deep_idle=off` and `y2.cpu_safe=1` retain fallback. New-image automatic cold boot is NOT_RUN.

| C3 prerequisite | Exact implemented and UART01-proved contract | Remaining scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Topology | CPU0 only; both exact secondary power copies 0; natural owner mask 0xe | Baseline02 affected regression |
| OPP | Entry ≤747.5 MHz; all five active OPPs retained and checked before/after | Mixed charging/thermal endurance |
| UART/clocks/domains | Normal MT6582 PIO SleepEN ownership, global SPM request bit 0 / ACK bit 20 before power-run/WFI; bounded ACK; no forced PERI gate | Baseline02 cold boot and wider activity |
| PCM | Exact 480-word MT6582 DPIDLE program, separate from system-suspend PCM; normal PCM restored | Wider wake/endurance |
| CPU context | Linux cpu_suspend/cpu_resume; real reset SRAM stamp, MMU/CP15/VFP/GIC, BIU/cache/coherency checked | Additional stress/endurance |
| CIRQ | Exact MT6582 clone/flush accounting matches 3,647 real returns; restore failures 0 | Injected pending-edge replay not exercised |
| GPT4/local timer | Sole broadcast owner, 13 MHz conversion, future deadline >2 ms/26,000 ticks; observed 3.471384–18.392615 ms; timer context restores match | Wider boundary coverage; 1 ms probes had no entry without isolating individual rejection |
| Wake and media | Same boot, responsive; screen/workload restore; eMMC and inserted SD integrity clean after C2/C3 | SD-absent variant, long endurance, full system suspend separate |

GPT6, architectural CNTFRQ/PPI29 on all cores, GPT4 sole broadcast, highres and NO_HZ pass. The UART1 clock is no longer an unresolved blocker. Read [UART implementation](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-C3-UART.md), [UART01 hardware](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-C3-UART-PHYSICAL.md), [SPM](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm.c), [runtime policy](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm-idle-policy.h), [PCM](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm-idle-pcm.h), [CIRQ](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/cirq.c), [local timer](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/local-timer.c) and [resume vector](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/resume-vector.S).

| Active OPP | Voltage | Bounded hardware scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 598 / 747.5 / 1040 MHz | 1.15 V | UART01 before/after C3 regression; earlier Fix02 integrity retained |
| 1196 MHz | 1.20 V | UART01 readback; earlier Fix02 integrity and 71.5°C peak retained |
| 1300 MHz | 1.25 V | UART01 readback; earlier guarded Fix02 load retained, without sustained thermal-envelope claim |

Schedutil, workload QoS, interactive boost, PWRAP voltage ordering and thermal authority remain. CPU/PMIC sensors are not pack sensors. The existing 110/120°C CPU and 150°C PMIC trips were not naturally exercised. Full same-boot system suspend with Power/RTC wake remains a separate unresolved release requirement.

## 6. STORAGE

### Retained storage-mode evidence and current integrity scope

| Host | Implemented maximum | Observed maximum | Qualified shipping basis | Proposed beta mode |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| eMMC | 8-bit HS200200MHz; DDR52; HS52;25/13MHz ladders | Candidate2~140MB/s128MiB direct read, tuning/readback pass, no CRC/timeout | Prior50MHz8-bit integrity/PM and earlier13MHz direct writes; not HS200 endurance | Highest robust measured HS200/DDR52/HS52; keep lower fallbacks |
| SD | 4-bit SDR104200MHz, DDR5050MHzDDR, SDR50100MHz, HS50/25/legacy13 | Candidate2 SDR104 starts, then2CRC errors→DDR50~40MB/s short read | Prior4-bit HS50 integrity/gating; few cards | Highest robust card-specific SDR104/SDR50/DDR50/HS; finish current ceiling work |

Prior HS50 qualification remains a fallback baseline, not the final target ceiling. Candidate3 reports clean100MHz reads and unstable200MHz; neither short reads nor old-setting failure settles the final card/board ceiling. Qualify the fastest robust mode after correct tuning/pads/card driver evaluation. Do not turn automatic downgrade after errors into a claim that starting every boot in a known-failing mode is an appropriate public policy.

Candidate3 source at `04b41b0` corrects the SD sweep from64 to32taps, prevents field spill into CLKTXDLY, scans both sampling edges with repeated successful tries, chooses the widest eye/margin, and rejects insufficient windows. Read-only `y2_storage_diag`/`y2_tune_diag` expose maps, picks, command/data/lane CRC counters, pad readbacks and first/last fault. The root-only `y2_lab` can mutate tuning/drive state and is currently compiled by header default `Y2_MSDC_LAB=1`; the proposed public profile compiles it out. Candidate3 sealed successfully and is now reported physically characterized in the now-committed report. 200MHz remains unstable across delay/drive sweeps; SDR104 timing capped100MHz passed2GiB at~42MB/s. SDR50 is also reported clean, without equivalent write/endurance proof. Candidate4 adds stock Schmitt inputs, zero RDSEL/TDSEL and card drive selection; candidate4 now has a sealed304-test software receipt but no physical result. Completion and qualification of the fastest robust production mode remain pre-release work. Sources: [tuning policy](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/storage-tuning.h), [MSDC patch](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/patches/0009-y2-msdc-readonly.patch), [test cases](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tests/test_sd_tuning.py), [Oct2 handoff](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2FHANDOFF-2026-10-02.md).

The ladder is eMMC HS200→DDR52→HS52→25→13; SD SDR104→DDR50→SDR50→HS50→25→13, matching core preference. Tuned-mode errors first retune then downgrade; voltage switch failures skip UHS. eMMC VCC is not power-cycled by Linux (no switchable VEMC declaration), VIO18 is fixed; SD VMCH/VMC admits only reviewed rail fields. A readback/fallback guard is useful but not proof of filesystem survival during every failed write. Current safety priority is keeping root/data and music intact.

### Filesystems and media lifecycle

Internal root/data are separate writable ext4 volumes with exact controller/geometry/UUID/schema validation. Initial preflight mounts `ro,noload`; subsequent unmounted `e2fsck -p` accepts only safe repairs before rw handoff. Uncorrectable errors stay in rescue; no autoformat, SD root substitution or reboot loop. SD ext4/vfat/exfat are enabled, with read-only filesystem checks and unique-volume selection; NTFS and ambiguous layouts are not product support. A historical dirty FAT card was isolated rather than repaired, and cannot count as current corruption caused by Linux.

Fix03 replaces reclaimable kernfs inode/ctime identity with `dev_t`, `diskseq` and CID. Fix02 had reproduced spurious SD unmount/remount on cache reclaim; that bug is coded as fixed, not physically closed. SD replacement, UUID-less FAT, reclaim, clean exFAT, safe eject and scan cancellation need one focused RC pass. Music-copy writes should use bounded private staging and verified hash/fsync publication; ordinary SFTP clients have reserve enforcement but lack the full atomic-publication contract. The96MiB/128-inode reserve is not protection against an unrestricted root administrator.

Sources: [media lifecycle](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/media.py), [space](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/space.py), [transfer](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/transfer.py), [storage contract](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Farchitecture%2Fplatform-storage-v1.md).

## 7. DISPLAY / GPU / INPUT

Native480×360 panel output uses Mediatek DRM/KMS, linearXRGB8888 scanout, GBM/EGL/GLES2 with Lima/kmsro on Mali-400MP2. Retained GPU01 reports EGL1.4, GLES2, Mali400 renderer,4096 maximum texture edge and16 texture units. This is hardware rendering, not llvmpipe; no production CPU-renderer fallback is selected. Static screens submit no unnecessary frames, dirty animation is capped about30FPS, and runtime power gating is physically observed. The awake GPU path is effectively finished for beta. Full-suspend context retention remains with the suspended-system gate, not a reason to rework Lima.

Input path: APT32F I2C frame → kernel key press/release → evdev → `InputManager` monotonic timestamp/dedupe/cadence → normalized actions → UI context policy. A same-direction interval under8ms is filtered; acceleration starts after four detents, with2/3/4-step thresholds at110/70/45ms and reset after220ms/reversal/loss/backwards time. Short menus stay one step; Now Playing wheel changes volume by2. This addresses the old1+2-row burst behavior in source; actual bounce statistics and physical detent acceptance are pending.

Select opens/acts and hold opens options. Back restores parent focus/scroll; hold returns Home. Play/Pause works globally; hold opens Now Playing. Previous/Next skip, holds seek±30s repeatedly. Volume repeats and works dark. Power toggles screen; hold opens Quick Settings. Dark navigation is dropped while transport/volume remain usable. [Current navigation](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FSchulzCode%2FY2Reborn%2Fblob%2Fmain%2Fdocs%2Fui%2FREBORN-PRODUCT-NAVIGATION-V2.md), [input source](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/crates/reborn-platform/src/input.rs).

Brightness is a real standard-backlight operation with five levels; shutdown closes graphics after a final black frame/backlight-off and platform independently darkens after app termination. These are implemented Productv2 changes, not a physical sign-off. Retain one human visual/control check for v2 rather than demand a UI redesign.

## 8. AUDIO

The complete native path is FFmpeg9.0.2 file demux/decode → shared float-planar DSP → conversion to actual sink format/rate → ALSA managed DMA → AFE DL1 → I05/I06→O00/O01 → second I2S → CS43131. AFE accepts S16_LE stereo at44100/48000. Both rates have short physical audio evidence; only44100 remains listed in `audio-qualified.json`, while `audio-enabled.json` enables both and marks candidate qualification false. This disagreement is intentional enablement versus evidence, not a reason to silently relabel48k qualified.

| Source input | Decoder/test coverage | Current wired output |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FLAC16/24,44.1/48/88.2/96 | Exact fixtures; float DSP and source metadata tests | 44.1-family→44.1k,48-family→48k, S16; ultimate44.1 fallback on open failure |
| MP3 | mp3/tone fixtures, seek/error handling | Converted to negotiated S16 sink |
| AAC raw /M4A AAC | aac.aac/m4a-aac.m4a | Same; independent of Bluetooth FDK encoder |
| ALAC/M4A | m4a-alac.m4a | Same |
| Vorbis/Ogg | vorbis.ogg/tone.ogg | Same |
| Opus | opus.opus/tone.opus | Same |
| WAV PCM16/24/32,float32 | Four exact PCM fixtures and truncated WAV | Same |
| AIFF/AIF PCM24 | aiff-pcm24.aiff | Same |
| APE | ape-silence.ape | Same |
| WavPack | wavpack-silence.wv | Same |

Per-format feature rows intentionally use SOFTWARE_VALIDATED unless a particular physical playback receipt proves more. Host/ARM fixture coverage is not a10-format physical listening pass. Scanner extensions are `flac mp3 aac m4a ogg opus wav aiff aif ape wv`; arbitrary FFmpeg codec availability is not exposed support. File protocol only; no FFmpeg network, encoders, video player or streaming app.

DSP includes live volume ramp, limiter/headroom, ReplayGain track/album/off, EQ bands, crossfade, gapless, resampling and channel/output conversion. EQ engine/settings exist but Productv2 exposes no editor and defaults off. Crossfade defaults0; gapless defaults on. Fix02 exercised5s crossfade with silent fixtures, which cannot certify audible boundaries. Regression tests cover volume partial writes/ramp,24-bit sign conversion, source metadata, delay/trim, artwork and malformed/truncated inputs. [Audio source](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/audio/mt6582-afe.c), [media tests](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/crates/reborn-media/src/native.rs), [DSP/volume](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/app/reborn/src/volume.rs).

CS43131 uses the upstream cs43130-family driver, DAPM, safe initialization/mute and runtime power sequencing; board machine code starts headphone pin disabled and upstream reset volume−60dB. User volume is the shared application DSP path with controlled native sink setup, not a new calibrated hardware-gain UI. Upstream exposes hardware volume/filter/phase/high-pass/de-emphasis controls; per-control use and qualification are absent. DT omits EINT16/load-measurement properties; jack/load/impedance-driven gain is not admitted without its independent board/load safety proof. No present DSD, speaker, nativeS24/S32, preserved24-in32 or88.2/96k implementation is claimed. Native high-resolution output is a release target with the hardware-proof gate below;32-bit serial slots alone prove none of it.

### Native high-resolution completion contract (F075/F076)

F075: FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET, NOT_IMPLEMENTED end to end, hardware-conditional. F076: CONDITIONAL_ON_HARDWARE, NOT_IMPLEMENTED. Reborn conversion helpers already support S24/S32 containers; no claim that all userspace work is absent. The missing native AFE path remains decisive.``

| Layer | Current fact | What must be proved/implemented |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Decoded source precision | FFmpeg decodes24/32-bit and88.2/96k sources, retains source metadata | Keep source format distinct from actual output, including UI reporting |
| DSP precision | Shared float-planar processing; floating point storage width is not a count of lossless output bits | Check low-level precision/headroom and clipping under selected DSP/bypass settings |
| ALSA format | Current hardware accepts S16_LE only; Reborn helpers expose S24_LE in4-byte containers and S32_LE | Select the exact supported format/alignment. S24_LE is not automatically packed S24_3LE; S32 container is not32 meaningful DAC bits |
| AFE memory fetch | DL1 presently consumes16-bit stereo; donor hd_reg=-1 | Find exact MT6582 wider fetch, signed alignment, ring/period accounting and I05/I06 interconnect precision |
| I2S slot width | 32-bit slots,64 BCLK/frame currently carry S16 payload | Capture exact bit placement/polarity/word length; padded slots are not high-resolution samples |
| Meaningful payload | Present native path preserves16 meaningful bits | Demonstrate meaningful low bits beyond16 with known24-bit fixtures; rule out truncation/zero padding before DAC |
| DAC input precision | CS43131 driver/capability does not establish delivered Y2 bus precision | Verify actual DAC serial configuration and received bitstream; no invented32-bit analog-resolution claim |
| Native rate/clock | AFE codes9/10 and rate masks only44.1/48; sources88.2→44.1 and96→48 today | Exact88.2/96 rate fields and MCLK/BCLK/LRCLK path; correct pitch/duration/sample count, no hidden resampling |

Missing technical evidence: exact MT6582 DL1 fetch/packing/interconnect and high-rate clock programming. The exact vendor header/donor limitations in [radio/audio audit](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-HARDWARE-FINAL-RADIO-AUDIO.md%23internal-wired-audio-and-cs43131&anchor=internal-wired-audio-and-cs43131) are insufficient evidence, not silicon-incapability proof. Investigate [AFE implementation](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/audio/mt6582-afe.c), [register/clock definitions](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/audio/mt6582-afe.h), [machine driver](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/audio/y2-cs43131.c), [media conversion](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/crates/reborn-media/native/media.c), [ALSA sink](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/crates/reborn-audio/native/audio.c) and [playback negotiation](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/app/reborn/src/playback.rs), against exact stock programming/MT6582 source.

Admission proof: authoritative register/stock evidence first, then low-bit DMA fixtures and measured I2S words/clocks,48/44.1/88.2/96 family transitions, channel order/sign, underruns, audible fidelity and sustained output. Missing capture access is an evidence/resource blocker, not permission to call S16 native24. If support remains unresolved, report the exact unresolved fields and delay an unconditional feature-complete claim. Only demonstrated hardware limitation permits a documented high-resolution exclusion.

## 9. BLUETOOTH

Native MT6627/CONSYS/firmware/BTIF produces standard HCI. BlueZ5.87 and BlueALSA5.0.0 provide Classic A2DP source; per-device calibration stays local. Pairing uses a bounded Pairable window, verifies bond, applies trust and explicitly connects. Reborn distinguishes connected/paired/bonded/audio-ready rather than enabling output solely from a link. `y2-bt-reconnect` is the only automatic reconnect owner; application actions hold operation leases. CONSYS has a bounded isolated retry, not an endless reset loop.

The strongest product audio receipt is182.955seconds of actual SBC playback with no XRUN/recovery/decode/playback errors and owner-confirmed clean AirPods audio/play-pause. Direct-fixture audio alone missed an application PCM-name bug later fixed. AVRCP player registration/metadata exists, but owner control observation and absence of an incoming retained trace must remain distinct. Fresh pair/reconnect/bond persistence, metadata, screen-off and coexistence remain RC tests. [Physical issues](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FPLATFORM-V1-PHYSICAL-ISSUES.md), [current contract](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Farchitecture%2Fplatform-bluetooth-v1.md).

| Codec | Compiled / encoder | Normal endpoint / Reborn | Peer/audio/CPU evidence | Fallback / distribution |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SBC | Yes, libsbc2.2, BlueALSA | Enabled; actual PCM shown | Short audible peer pass, no completed long endurance | Mandatory baseline; package notices needed |
| SBC XQ | SBC quality preference/policy support | Private high/xq/xq+ controls; quality default high; negotiated peer acceptance separate | No XQ-specific negotiated playback/CPU evidence | Standard SBC; not a separate codec license |
| AAC | FDK-AAC2.0.3; BlueALSA built | Enabled by current owner-private integration policy; not publicly approved | ARM help/linkage, no retained physical AAC pass | SBC; FDK notice/source/patent review unresolved |
| aptX | libfreeaptx0.2.2; BlueALSA built | Current owner-private integration endpoint enabled; public profile gated | No compatible-peer/CPU result located | SBC; LGPL source duties and product rights review |
| aptX HD | Same library; own endpoint | Current owner-private integration endpoint enabled; public profile gated | No peer/CPU result located | SBC; same review |
| LDAC | libldac encoder+ABR2.0.2.3 | Current owner-private integration endpoint enabled; public profile gated | No peer/quality/CPU run located | SBC; certification NOTICE unresolved |

LDAC quality rates are330/660/990kbps in48k family,303/606/909 in44.1 family. ABR being linked is not measured dynamic bitrate. Auto has no qualified optional codecs; preferred codec is never equated with negotiated codec. Optional encoders run on CPU; controller version does not itself prohibit those Classic codecs. LE Audio, aptX Adaptive/Lossless have no supported current path and are not beta gaps.

Shared-radio coexistence must test SBC with Wi-Fi idle/scan/transfer, not assume separate-link success. No sustained simultaneous acceptance receipt was found. Standard SBC and possibly Wi-Fi-off-during-playback documentation are defensible fallbacks if the limited concurrency test finds a non-core limitation; repeated ordinary SBC failure remains a blocker.

### Classic codec feature-completion acceptance

SBC/XQ/AAC/aptX/HD/LDAC, LDAC quality modes and ABR are release targets. AAC/aptX/HD/LDAC are technically viable host encoders over Classic A2DP, with private build/BlueALSA support; real peer/resource envelopes remain unqualified. Current private endpoint enablement is separate from peer qualification and public-distribution permission.

| Codec/mode | Code | Encoder built | BlueALSA support | Default endpoint/mode | Peer-tested | Audible on Y2 | Codec CPU acceptance | Coexistence acceptance | Public distribution |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SBC | Yes | libsbc2.2 | Yes | Enabled | Bounded named peer | Yes, short owner observation | No codec-specific sustained receipt | No release endurance | OSS notices/source review |
| SBC XQ | Quality/policy exists | Shares libsbc | Quality option | XQ off; SBC endpoint on | Not evidenced | Not evidenced forXQ | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Same component; peer interoperability separate |
| AAC | Yes | FDK2.0.3 private | Yes | Owner-private enabled; public gated | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | DISTRIBUTION_BLOCKED_OR_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| aptX | Yes | freeaptx0.2.2 private | Yes | Owner-private enabled; public gated | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | DISTRIBUTION_BLOCKED_OR_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| aptX HD | Yes | freeaptx0.2.2 private | Yes | Owner-private enabled; public gated | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | DISTRIBUTION_BLOCKED_OR_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| LDAC | Yes | 2.0.2.3 private | Yes | Owner-private enabled; public gated | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | Not evidenced | DISTRIBUTION_BLOCKED_OR_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| LDAC quality modes | Yes | Same encoder | mobile/standard/high controls | Owner-private controls enabled; unqualified | Not evidenced per mode | Not evidenced per mode | Not evidenced | Not evidenced per mode | Same LDAC gate |
| LDAC ABR | Yes | ABR library linked | ABR argument supported | Owner-private policy default; adaptation unqualified | No adaptation receipt | No ABR listening receipt | Not evidenced | No adaptation-under-load proof | Same LDAC gate |

Each codec needs: peer capability discovery → intersection with built/enabled/approved local codecs → persisted user preference/Auto → bounded selection/retry → actual negotiated PCM and audible playback → fallback/reconnect → CPU/temperature/dropout evidence with Wi-Fi idle/scan/transfer. Auto may select only mutually available admitted codecs; no best-codec claim from preference alone. Quality labels must distinguish requested target from measured bitrate; ABR needs actual adaptation evidence, not a checkbox. Named peer limits can remain documented. An unavailable compatible peer blocks that codec's acceptance, not its release priority.

Sources: [manifest](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/production/codec_manifest.py), [pinned encoder/license ledger](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/production/codec-sources.json), [service arguments](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/buildroot/board/y2/production-overlay/usr/libexec/y2/connectivity), [observed BT state](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/bluetooth.py), [Reborn codec policy](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/crates/reborn-platform/src/codecs.rs). Existing notices identify unresolved distribution review; this document makes no new legal clearance claim. A legally forced public omission must remain FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET with a named distribution exception and a clearly disclosed delivered feature set.

## 10. WI-FI

Current source has firmware/native driver, nl80211 supplicant, scan/password/saved-network UI, persistent private credentials, WPA2-PSK/CCMP association, DHCP hook, route/DNS readiness, NTP and reconnect/power policy. The real TX-checksum defect was fixed: unsupported `NETIF_F_HW_CSUM` advertisement removed, ordinary checked DNS/TCP then passed. Do not repeat the old checksum investigation.

Physical01 local throughput: receive45.15–45.60Mb/s, transmit46.35–46.56Mb/s across three repeats. Fix02 adds four1MiB SHA roundtrips each direction. These are separate from screen-off/coexistence endurance. Supplicant STATUS timeouts can produce transient unavailable telemetry while traffic works; Reborn now preserves last known association for counter accounting while reporting observation failure truthfully. Do not increase timeouts or falsify Online solely to make a counter green. Current UI password/forget/reconnect and screen-off behavior need RC checks. Enterprise/WPA3/AP/P2P/WPS are not supported product promises.

## 11. USB

Production MUSB supplies high-speed ACM+ECM. Device address10.42.0.1/24, host commonly10.42.0.2/24; file transfer is authenticated SFTP, not a mounted disk/MTP. Dropbear binds both10.42.0.1:22 andusb0, disables passwords/forwarding, waits for CRNG and owner public key. Failed interface binding closes the socket. Only OpenSSH's SFTP subsystem is installed, no second sshd. The fixed transfer helper stages privately, validates path/mount generation/space, seals a fresh inode, hashes and fsyncs publication; ordinary SFTP has weaker partial-upload semantics.

UART01 supersedes the older Fix02 loaded-transfer failure for the repaired source: three 1 MiB bidirectional checksum rounds pass with an independent Wi-Fi observer, same boot and no filesystem errors. Baseline02 retains the progress-aware DMA protection. This closes the bounded integrity regression, while large transfers, cable/reconnect/host-sleep endurance and supported-host coverage remain separate. PIO remains the supported fallback; no new candidate series is needed merely to retain it. [Hardware receipt](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-C3-UART-PHYSICAL.md).

NCM modules and host classes are compiled, but activation is gated. ID wiring, VBUS source/switch/current are not board-qualified. Host-role requests fail closed before VBUS writes. HID/storage/UAC availability in config is not host usability. Keep peripheral ECM; support a documented host OS first, with Windows ECM setup explicitly unqualified until tested. [USB contract](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Farchitecture%2Fplatform-usb-v1.md), [Fix03 source/evidence](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-FINAL-FIX03.md).

## 12. BATTERY / CHARGING

Voltage/presence are real ADC observations. SOC is a filtered voltage curve3.4–4.175V,30s filtering,120s rest criterion and charging offset; not a calibrated fuel gauge. No measured battery current, coulomb count, capacity or pack temperature is provided. Stock FG routines returning zero and constant25°C reports are not measurements. Show percentage only with a visible estimated label, retain low-voltage warning, never show remaining hours or calibrated accuracy.

Current charger source admits70/450/650mA selectors, with bounded source classification; the observed PC500mA allowance selects450mA. Default configurable ceiling650mA is a maximum, not measured input or battery current. CV4.175V, sampled4.2V safety guard, recharge4.110V, top-off4.050V,3h precharge/CV and24h session guards, charger watchdog and stock OVP/UVLO/PMIC die protections remain. Boot threshold3.2V, precharge boundary3.4V, and software critical-shutdown3.4V are different policies; do not confuse them.

Existing history shows charging voltage rises and hold/recharge states, but no controlled latest-candidate full-cycle/combined-use acceptance.1121.57s rising3.540→3.574V under changing load does not prove positive net charge for all workloads. PMIC/CPU temperature is not pack temperature. The audit cannot currently say every beta user can charge normally without special qualification. One attended ordinary PC and wall-charge session with playback/idle, credible external input observation and hold/recharge behavior is a release gate. Do not increase current or probe an unknown pack's absolute discharge limit.

Low-battery policy is enabled/provisional:3.5V low,3.4V critical,3.6V recovery, five critical samples and10s grace. Provisional SOC warns, calibrated-confidence gating prevents blindly treating estimated5% as hardware capacity. Platform shutdown is independent of app: peer-credential checked socket, one monotonic deadline, stop audio/save session/stop scan/checkpoint-close DB, bounded stop/kill, journal/sync and standard BusyBox shutdown. Kernel uninterruptible I/O and actual PMIC power cut cannot be guaranteed by a userspace timeout. Test reserve and next-boot integrity; do not equate a final journal record with physical off. Sources: [policy](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/charger-policy.h), [power service](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/power.py), [SOC](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/platform/y2_platform/battery.py).

### Battery feature completion versus unavailable measurements

Useful honest percentage, charging state, low/critical warning, safe persistence/shutdown and ordinary charging are core release requirements. Calibrated SOC is not the same as a voltage-derived estimate. F180 is now CONDITIONAL_ON_HARDWARE: real current/coulomb/pack-temperature completion must be investigated if exact MT6323/Y2 wiring supports it.

Current [ADC source](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/mt6323-adc.c) exposes BATSNS/ISENSE voltages and BATON voltage; it deliberately does not turn an unproven68mΩ shunt/offset/polarity or unverified NTC into amps/°C. Stock zero-return FG and constant-temperature behavior do not prove a usable coulomb counter. Missing proof is the exact wired sense/coulomb/NTC path, calibration and net-current sign under charge/discharge. Require comparison to appropriate measurement; CPU/PMIC die readings and configured charger current are not substitutes. If only useful estimated SOC is viable, explicitly document that proven limitation; do not invent current or classify the unresolved hardware inquiry as post-beta because it is difficult.

## 13. RTC / SUSPEND

RTC reads/write/ticking and NTP→RTC writeback have physical observations; rtc-policy now enables writes. Reboot and cold-power retention remain explicit tests. Awake RTC alarm+PMIC IRQ passed. Full sleep is different: Fix02 freezer/devices/platform/processors/core stages pass on battery; active charging refuses with clean−EBUSY unwind. Full RTC sleep returns from SPM, stamps resume vector, restores syscore/secondaries/device callbacks, then misses PM exit and resets via30s backstop. Userspace/radio restoration never completes. Power wake and five cycles are untested.

Fix03 adds timed breadcrumbs through the remaining resume-console/platform-end/thaw/notifier window, not a proven fix for the unknown stalled call. Warm SRAM journal self-test/retention is qualified; cold power loses it. Deep suspend is a CORE_RELEASE_FEATURE. Repair the remaining return-to-userspace failure and integrate Power→real sleep→Power/RTC wake→same boot with actual subsystem restoration. Current Power screen-toggle behavior is not that target. Runtime screen-off and real Power Off remain safe fallbacks, not completion waivers. If same-boot sleep remains broken, delay the feature-complete beta.

### Full sleep/wake acceptance ledger (F032–F036, F065, F191, F231)

Target: deliberate Power action → real low-power sleep → very low observed drain → Power or RTC wake → same boot → restored UI/audio/radios/storage. Playback-active policy must be deliberate and user-visible: screen-off playback is a distinct mode; pause/checkpoint before full sleep where continuous output cannot survive system suspend. Existing short Power screen-toggle and long-hold Quick Settings are current behavior, not proof of the target. Final interaction choice belongs in Pass3 integration with the platform sleep contract.

| Stage/subsystem | Retained fact | Required release proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Freezer | Fix02 pm_test pass | Affected regression and successful final thaw |
| Devices stage | Fix02 staged pass | Actual sleep cycle does not leave devices unusable |
| Platform/processors/core stages | Fix02 staged passes | Full transition, topology and same-boot return |
| SPM entry | Full attempt reached sleep/wake path | Real residence and wake reason; not a staged test |
| CPU resume | Resume vector/SPM return stamped | Stable userspace completion, no30s backstop reset |
| RTC wake | Wake0x20/return observed; full completion failed | Alarm wakes to working product, repeatably |
| Power wake | No completed physical receipt | Button IRQ wake, same boot, no lost/stuck input |
| CIRQ replay | Code/breadcrumb path exists | Pending wake interrupts replay correctly; no duplicate/lost event |
| Timer restore | Syscore/device progress before failure | Monotonic time/deadlines and each online CPU clockevent after return |
| Storage restore | Callbacks completed in failed overall run | Root/data/SD readable/writable, hashes/fsync, chosen fast timing retained/retrained safely |
| USB restore | No final product resume proof | Host sees recoverable ACM/ECM, authenticated transfer resumes/reconnects with hash integrity |
| Wi-Fi restore | No same-boot final product proof | Association/DHCP/route/DNS restored by bounded owner workflow |
| Bluetooth restore | No same-boot final product proof | Bond/trust/connection/negotiated codec and audible playback restored without competing reconnect owners |
| GPU/display restore | Awake/runtime PM mature; full sleep unqualified | Correct first resumed frame/backlight, no white screen/hang |
| ALSA restore | No final full-sleep audio proof | AFE/I2S/DAC reconfiguration, mute/pop behavior and correct selected output |
| Reborn restore | Full run resets before completed userspace return | Input/focus/queue/settings/worker liveness, deliberate paused/resume policy |
| Sleep drain | No completed measured deep-sleep envelope | Comparable awake/sleep observation and measured consumption where available; define numeric target from actual evidence, no invented hours |

Fix03 instrumentation must identify the first uncompleted console/platform-end/thaw/notifier step. Fix the observed stall before expanding cycles. Preserve SRAM journal, stage timestamps and watchdog reset attribution. A callback-return breadcrumb alone does not qualify the corresponding restored device. Charge-active refusal/clean unwind is a safety contract; test deliberate charging behavior rather than bypassing it. Missing final same-boot wake is a release blocker, not an acceptable screen-off substitute.

## 14. BUILDROOT / PLATFORM SERVICES

Buildroot2025.02.18, pinned external Bootlin ARMv7 hard-float glibc toolchain, BusyBox init, util-linux/e2fsprogs/dosfstools/exfatprogs, ALSA, Python standard modules, SQLite, wpa_supplicant, D-Bus/BlueZ/BlueALSA, libcurl/OpenSSL/CA certificates and Mesa/Lima are deliberately selected. Diagnostics include evtest, DRM/ALSA queries, i2c-tools, ethtool/iw, strace, iperf3 and memtester. Their existence is not permission to expose arbitrary hardware mutation to ordinary testers.

Startup sequence: RAM init mounts observation filesystems and modules → charging/normal-boot gate → internal identity/fsck → switch_root → runtime/data/platform/time/power setup → S05Reborn → later connectivity/readiness/time/SSH services. Reborn radio workers retry; first frame need not wait for radios. Readiness/status distinguish available implementations from live device state. Bounded supervisors record exhausted restart budgets.

The Reborn platform crate owns Y2 CLI/records/sysfs/power/workload operations. Typed models reach UI; one worker refreshes only visible platform pages,15s normally/4s PC Transfer, never screen-off. Battery record is read every2s. Application-owned standard APIs remain FFmpeg/ALSA, SQLite, DRM/GBM/EGL, evdev via platform, own process/mount diagnostics, own state/control socket. Rust guard's10 allowlist tuples and C graphics splash protocol are explicit exceptions, not evidence of a new platform-layer violation. Core session-file I/O is app-owned despite overly broad wording in the older boundary doc. No architectural rewrite is needed for beta.

## 15. REBORN CORE

SQLite schema1 uses a single worker/WAL, compatible browse indexes, transactional scan results, source generation/claims and safe pruning. Proven corruption is quarantined together with WAL/SHM and interrupted moves recover; future-schema/locked/full/read-only failures must not be mistaken for corruption or silently wiped. Rebuild now forces reread; incremental reuse uses size/mtime. Metadata/artwork errors retain usable songs. Source loss marks availability rather than deleting music.

Matched physical benchmarks on the older8-bit13MHz transport:

| Tracks | Indexed page p50/p99 ms | Commit64 p99 ms | Initial/incremental scan seconds |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1k | 2.384/2.860 | 6.984 | 4.154/0.192 |
| 10k | 2.067/2.428 | 578.580 | 52.553/7.666 |
| 20k | 2.080/2.448 | 562.513 | 114.573/18.251 |

These synthetic results prove meaningful usable scale, not all real-world metadata/artwork latency. Full list construction~45ms and scan CPU remain costs. The source already corrected the earlier~95ms20k browse page and multi-second one-bit WAL stalls. No additional database redesign is warranted. [Benchmark evidence](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FPLATFORM-V1-HARDWARE-CEILING.md).

Playback has decoder worker, bounded PCM buffering/writes, generation cancellation, serialized transactional sink changes, queue occurrence IDs, seek trimming, gapless/crossfade and error recovery. A full producer queue increments `decoder_stalls`; that is backpressure, not necessarily underrun. Report actual `audio_xruns`, recovery/write/decode errors and audible behavior separately. Queue entries may outlive a removed file; current Fix02 cleanup left fixture entries, so source loss/skip/clear is a useful RC scenario, not a reason to rewrite queue identity.

Settings/session atomic writes and serde defaults preserve current fields; there is no broad versioned settings-migration framework. Database schema remains1; update verifier rejects incompatible destructive schema combinations. Preserve `/data/reborn`, network credentials and Bluetooth bonds across beta updates. Text search backend exists, but no dedicated current search screen; Favorites and persistent playlist editing are absent. They remain nonessential extensions to the current deliberately simplified Productv2 direction, rather than hardware limitations; the owner-requested audio/power/storage features are not in this category.

## 16. REBORN UI

Every current route has a matrix row. Productv2 has27 normal routes in its review count, four root items and a technical Diagnostics subtree. Music: Albums/Album, Artists/Artist, Songs, Folders, letter index; playback: Now Playing/options, Queue/options, Song Info; Settings: Wi-Fi/password, Bluetooth/pairing, PC Transfer, Audio/output/ReplayGain, Playback, Library, Display, System/Battery/Storage/Update/About/maintenance. Hold Power opens Quick Settings, restart/off confirmed. No separate Connectivity hub or Favorites screen; Quick Settings is a sheet, not a dead old route.

Maintenance offers real app-settings reset, library rebuild, artwork-cache clear, forget Wi-Fi and forget Bluetooth; full wipe is absent from normal UI. Facts are static rather than focusable on ordinary pages; full values and hardware details live in Diagnostics. Old reset pages printing shell commands were removed. Empty states name no music, failed/incomplete scan, missing SD, no current track or no queue and provide useful actions. Long labels/Unicode/focus restoration have tests/previews; all require one human480×360 check.

Two practical product gaps matter more than polish: PC Transfer hides the SFTP/address/auth mechanism in Diagnostics, so a fresh tester needs a visible route to instructions; Export Player Data is a private state backup, not a shareable bug bundle. License page has summary copy claiming system license texts; audit packaging should verify actual installed/shipped notices, since Reborn recipe visibly installs its own LICENSE/dependencies only. No evidence justifies wholesale UI work.

Bootv2 shows the same Reborn mark/background in initramfs and app with READY/RELEASED/PRESENTED handoff, then six-frame~200ms dissolve. Stock LK logo remains untouched. Shutdown uses~0.9s animation, closes audio/database/state, black frame, backlight off, acknowledgement, exit; platform darkens independently if app fails. Older52s first-frame measurement predates lazy FFmpeg and early app startup. Neither later~14.5s SSH recovery nor preview rendering proves latest first-frame timing or absence of white flash. Record one current video/timing sequence.

## 17. UPDATES / RECOVERY

Signed root-only OTA is substantial software: exact-byte manifest Ed25519, product/revision/source/kernel/schema/size/hash binding, bounded HTTPS/local staging, offline rescue backup of actual ext4 extent, fsync/readback verification, durable journal, high-water sequence, health ACK and verified previous-root restore. Pending health requires actual first frame/app liveness/platform checks within180s; missing ACK rolls back on next owner reboot, not a reboot loop. Reborn is updated as part of root; changed kernel/DT/modules require paired manual BOOTIMG+root.

Physical installation/interruption/restore of this integrated updater is not qualified. Current registry is a development key allowing explicitly signed development downgrade. Public channel is not configured; no autonomous network update source is guessed. Root-only update cannot rotate/revoke keys embedded in immutable rescue. Automatic BOOTIMG OTA and A/B are absent by design.

For beta1, computer-assisted preserving beta2 updates are acceptable after a rehearsal preserving settings/library/Wi-Fi/bonds. Do not make a new OTA server or physical torn-write campaign mandatory. Public UI must not invite an unqualified root writer; disable/hide OTA or retain explicit experimental gating unless fully qualified. A bad BOOTIMG cannot use Linux rescue; external retained stock loader/SPFT is the recovery path. Bad root can stay in rescue, but RAM rescue has ACM logging/console rather than ordinary USB SSH. Failed app recovery relies on the supervisor and owner USB access.

Current preserving package has BOOTIMG+ANDROID/Y2ROOT only, no Y2DATA payload. It is not a complete first installation for another stock owner. First use must deliberately initialize Android USRDATA as Y2DATA and provision that owner's public key, with backup/data-loss explanation; future updates must exclude it. `owner_data.py` exists but requires a historical boot-only system/base relationship and cannot simply accept the latest preserving package unchanged. This is concrete release-tooling work, not a missing kernel feature. Use current generated coordinate metadata; the historical recovery guide contains old logical/native readback assumptions and must not be copied blindly.

## 18. SECURITY / PRIVACY

Source audit found no universal production password, automatic Wi-Fi SSH listener or HTTP upload endpoint. Root password is locked; Dropbear key-only USB/interface-bound; no port forwarding; CRNG readiness required. Host keys generated per device, persistent on Y2DATA; authorizations must be per owner. Private candidate/provisioning artifacts must still be scanned before publication: safe source defaults do not prove a private image contains no maintainer state. A default credential or unexpected reachable endpoint is an unconditional A blocker.

Reborn and most platform services run as root; media parsing is not sandboxed, no LSM/KASLR/verified boot is added. This is a trusted single-owner local-media beta model, not security certification. Keep FFmpeg network disabled, use maintained pinned dependencies and a targeted release advisory review, verify sockets/permissions and no secrets/calibration/host keys in public output. Broad privilege separation is post-beta; a concrete exploitable release defect would override that deferral.

Privacy: no analytics or automatic log upload was found in the project source paths. The system does contact external services when connected: DNS readiness lookup of `pool.ntp.org`, NTP peers `0/1/2.pool.ntp.org`, and explicit owner-configured HTTPS update requests. Network peers see ordinary IP/radio identity; Wi-Fi/BT operations expose their normal link identifiers. Opaque firmware behavior is not audited internally. State backup contains library metadata/paths and preferred-device identity; optional network export can include credentials. Reborn diagnostic redaction/bounds have tests, but platform raw logs/CID/addresses must be reviewed in a unified shareable bundle. Never describe “no analytics” as “no external traffic.”

## 19. RELEASE / DISTRIBUTION

### Current build and package boundary

Baseline02 is the latest sealed owner-local candidate; its retained software checks and source pair supersede older integration counts in the historical receipt below. New-image/cold-boot hardware is PHYSICAL_NOT_RUN. See [Baseline02](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-BASELINE-02.md).

### Historical integration build receipt — 2026-10-02

Pinned kernel/Buildroot/toolchain/Rust archives, Cargo.lock/vendor checksums, FFmpeg pin, local package hashes, source bundles, ELF closure and exact manifests provide strong reconstruction. Candidate2 records281 production tests with3 explicit native-dependency skips,212 Reborn tests, ARM/QEMU, installed ARM imports and package/ELF/legal-info passes. Candidate3 subsequently records301 production/platform tests (3 native-dependency skips) and passing ARM/QEMU, installed ARM, ELF, inventory/legal-info/package checks; unchanged Reborn cargo checks were not rerun. Candidate4 now has304 passing software tests and a sealed receipt at e2dd583; no physical result. Software receipts are not physical qualification. `BR2_REPRODUCIBLE=y` is set; byte-identical rebuilding has not been demonstrated. One clean reproducible procedure and retained inputs/versions is a realistic beta bar; a two-build byte-equality project is not required this week.

The public preserving scatter must select only BOOTIMG and ANDROID, with PRELOADER, LK/UBOOT, MBR/EBRs, NVRAM, PROTECT, calibration, RECOVERY/LOGO and USRDATA excluded. The first-install data initializer must be separate and unmistakably destructive to prior Android userdata. Preserve stock loaders and protected records on each device. Never bundle someone else's radio addresses/calibration or a private raw evidence directory. Manual package SHA256 is integrity metadata, not release authentication or secure boot; publish it through the authenticated release channel and provide corresponding source/notices.

### Distribution review by component

“Likely safe” below means an ordinary open-source redistribution path appears available subject to license obligations, not legal clearance. The relevant primary notices were checked during the audit. [Buildroot's manual](https://buildroot.org/downloads/manual/manual.html#legal-info) describes legal-info as assistance, not a complete compliance guarantee; local collector warnings remain unresolved.[](https://ffmpeg.org/legal.html)[](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mstorsjo/fdk-aac/v2.0.3/NOTICE)[](https://android.googlesource.com/platform/external/libldac/+/2efdd91222c4c5f929335f34cbc3b576343cf1d2/NOTICE)``

| Component | Evidence / license family | Disposition for beta |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Linux6.18 + platform/patches | GPL-2.0-only/syscall exception, per-file SPDX and source manifest | Likely ordinary redistribution path; ship corresponding source/config/patch/build material |
| Buildroot2025.02.18 | GPL-2.0 build system; each target package separate | Supply own modifications and actual target/source obligations; collector does not automatically save everything |
| Bootlin toolchain / glibc / GCC runtime | Pinned external archive; collector warns about external license files | Requires closure of exact source/notices/license files, not blanket GPL label |
| Reborn and its assets/dependencies | MIT workspace; DejaVu and icon/font provenance files; Cargo dependency licenses | Likely ordinary path; bundle notices and verify all artwork/asset provenance, including generated mark and any retained reference assets |
| FFmpeg9.0.2 | Actual configured libraries; no external encoders/network; upstream LGPL default, GPL components conditional | Verify actual configure/license inventory; source and relinking obligations as applicable. FFmpeg legal page |
| OpenSSL | Candidate2 inventory: OpenSSL3.5.8, Apache-2.0 | Retain exact version notices; no inference from tool name alone |
| BlueZ5.87 | GPL/LGPL split by component | Corresponding source and notices; inspect selected files |
| BlueALSA5.0.0 | MIT core plus separately licensed encoders | Core redistribution path does not approve linked optional codecs |
| libsbc2.2 | LGPL family | Source/notices for shipped library |
| FDK-AAC2.0.3 | FDK license; notice/source requirements; no patent license granted | Required feature target; distribution review unresolved. Any public exclusion must be recorded as a legal/distribution exception, not technical deferral. FFmpeg AAC file decoding is separate. Pinned NOTICE |
| libfreeaptx0.2.2 / aptX / aptXHD | LGPL-2.1-or-later shared implementation | Copyright/source path exists; product rights/trademark/patent questions not settled by legal-info; exclude private experiment profile for now |
| LDAC encoder/ABR2.0.2.3 | Apache2 source plus certification NOTICE | Requires review; do not bundle merely because endpoint is off. Pinned AOSP NOTICE |
| Mesa/Lima/libdrm | Mesa MIT/SGI/Khronos inventory, Lima per-file GPL/MIT, libdrm MIT | Ordinary open-source path; no proprietary Mali userspace needed |
| Wi-Fi/BT/WMT firmware + stock defaults | Seven owner-provisioned hash-checked files; redistribution_permission_established=false | Owner-only / cannot bundle yet absent permission; establish reviewed permission or owner-local extraction/assembly workflow |
| Per-device NVRAM/calibration/addresses | Device-owned private factory records | Never redistribute as a generic template; preserve/acquire on the owning device only |
| Stock preloader/LK/ROM/logo/recovery binaries | Intentionally retained on device; private fallback/stock inputs not licensed by project | Do not include them in public package without established rights; owner-local recovery assets |
| SP Flash Tool / download agent | Proven owner tooling, not newly licensed project code | Document exact supported provenance; do not casually redistribute executable/tool bundle |

Evidence: [reconstruction boundary](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fbuild%2Fplatform-v1-reconstruction.md), [codec source ledger](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/production/codec-sources.json), [provisioning](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/tools/connectivity/provision.py), [Reborn dependency manifest](https://github.com/SchulzCode/Y2Reborn/blob/main/docs/architecture/dependencies.json). Offline/package legal-info success is useful evidence but not a final public-distribution verdict. No legal determination beyond these source notices is made.

Hardware support is one observed Innioasis Y2 MT6582/eastaeon82_wet_kk configuration, with particular eMMC geometry, panel, MT6627, CS43131 and battery assumptions. The repositories do not establish a complete list of commercial Y2 revisions. Do not assert all Y2s are identical or that another revision is known unsafe; both would exceed evidence. Beta compatibility must name accepted checks and reject unexpected identity/layout rather than guessing rail/display/partition compatibility.

## 20. TEST / QUALIFICATION

### Coverage and limits

Current Baseline02 receipts: 391 production cases (386 pass, 5 dependency skips covered by 27 native filesystem/GIO, 3 ALSA and ARM checks), 125 focused source tests and 238 Reborn tests; fresh kernel/root/ARM/QEMU/ELF/config/DT/ABI/package PASS. This documentation update runs documentation/structure checks only. The older counts below belong to the dated census/implementation campaign.

The historical 2026-10-02 census scanned all tracked text across both repos, including non-obvious tools, vendored Wi-Fi and Rust dependencies, docs, validation and historical evidence. Generated build/worktree copies are not counted as independent source; selected manifests and physical summaries were inspected read-only. The JSON retains exact text-file paths and marker/gate line numbers. This is an exhaustive capability map and lexical census, not formal verification of every dependency line, a live electrical audit, or an exhaustive CVE/legal opinion.

Existing tests include pure policy C harnesses, Python unit/integration, native verifier/writer fault tests, Rust/native media/SQLite/sink/input/UI tests, ARM QEMU, kernel/DT/config/ABI/memory/image gates, package rejection and ELF closure. Some tests model hardware rather than executing it. The earlier `test_baseline.py` receipt is historically scoped outside the production suite; current Baseline02 dependency skips and compensating checks are listed above. A test skip must retain its reason and compensating coverage, never be counted as pass. Candidate3 has a completed301-test software receipt with3 explicit skips. The newer candidate4 receipt reports304 software tests PASS; no hardware pass follows from that.

### Retained performance and endurance map

| Area | Actual retained evidence | Missing beta-relevant evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Boot | Old first frame52.060s; later~14.5s to SSH | Latest v2 first-frame and no-SD startup measurement |
| UI/wheel | Dirty rendering≤30FPS policy; earlier hardware renderer; real input boost~22ms | Latest human detent/focus latency;22ms lease is not end-to-end wheel latency |
| Library | 1k/10k/20k measured table above | Realistic mixed library on selected RC mode |
| Wired | 1min each44.1/48 +6 switches; later25s silent app windows | Long real playback and audible boundaries |
| Bluetooth | ~183s SBC actual app, owner clean/control | 2h SBC plus fresh pair/reconnect and coexistence |
| Screen-off | UART01 C2 84.3473%, C3 65.2450% separate 60-second windows; repeated actual returns, natural parking | New Baseline02 automatic cold boot; wider endurance/electrical drain; no battery-duration promise |
| Wi-Fi | ~45–47Mb/s standalone;4×1MiB hashes | Screen-off/concurrent audio continuity |
| USB | UART01 three 1 MiB bidirectional checksum rounds; independent Wi-Fi observer | Baseline02 affected regression plus large-transfer/reconnect/host-sleep endurance |
| eMMC | Safe-mode read/write/gating hashes; HS200128MiB read~140MB/s | Selected highest-robust mode mixed reads/writes/fsync/sleep/endurance; HS200 must be evaluated |
| SD | Older integrity passes; candidate2 SDR104 CRC→DDR50; candidate3 reported100MHz2GiB clean~42MB/s,200MHz still CRC | Multiple clean filesystems/cards, highest-robust-mode writes/hotplug/sleep and verified lower fallbacks |
| Thermal | Cooling authority;1300 four-worker75°C guard reached quickly | Ordinary charging+playback+radio/scan acceptable envelope |
| Suspend | Staged same-boot pass; one failed full wake/backstop reset | Required same-boot Power/RTC wake and restoration/cycle qualification |
| Update | Real host crypto/writer failure injection | Physical OTA recovery if promoted; otherwise manual upgrade rehearsal |

No completed release-representative long wired, Bluetooth, screen-off, USB or storage endurance receipt was located. Existing `wired-8h`, `bluetooth-8h`, coexistence, transfer, boot and suspend profiles are test tooling, not test results. Required feature-completion suite and pass/stop criteria are in the [plan](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-COMMUNITY-BETA-PLAN.md%23top-10-physical-qualification-tasks&anchor=top-10-physical-qualification-tasks).

### TODO / FIXME / marker disposition

The machine inventory classifies each lexical hit by file and line: dependency debt, historical/documentary claim, test fixture/negative assertion, vendor Wi-Fi reachability debt, or current gate/fallback/operational-state review. “TEMP” sensor constants, architecture exception strings, `STUBS` in a C test harness, “Unavailable” for absent hardware and a negative UI test are not missing product features.

Meaningful classes: vendor Wi-Fi unfinished RX counters/Host-FW synchronization/AP/P2P handlers are deferred unless a reached client-mode failure implicates them; OTP/recursive erase refusal in `fs-store.h` is a deliberate boundary, not a feature to complete; explicit unqualified suspend/codec/native-audio gates remain; real release gaps are tracked by independent product targets and completion groups. UIv2 generic-unavailable cleanup is implemented, not an outstanding instruction to prune again. The complete marker census is searchable without turning every inherited TODO into a week's work.

### Runtime and compile-time gate decisions````````````````````````````````

| Gate | Current effect | Proposed beta disposition / accessibility |
| --- | --- | --- |
| y2.cpu_safe=1 | Disables DVFS/idle/deep/suspend paths via common safe predicate | Retain rescue option, not default product profile; prebuilt tested fallback easier for tester |
| y2.dvfs=off | Disables platform DVFS admission; exact meaning from cpu-options | Retain diagnostic escape; no promise it selects an arbitrary frequency |
| y2.local_timer=off | Forces legacy timer fallback | Retain, normally leave qualified local timers on |
| y2.cpuidle=off | Disables board idle driver | Retain fault isolation; higher drain expected |
| y2.deep_idle=off | Disables C3 only; C2 remains available | Retain recovery option; Baseline02 startup otherwise automatically admits qualified CPU0 C3 |
| y2.suspend_safe=1 | Refuses full platform suspend | Retain emergency/qualification fallback; normal product must support qualified deep sleep |
| y2.emmc_mode=HS52 | Caps eMMC to preceding HS52 policy | Retain fallback; final default is fastest robust qualified mode |
| y2.sd_mode=HS | Skips UHS to3.3V high speed | Retain fallback; final default is fastest robust qualified mode |
| y2.mmc_safe=1 | Crystal26MHz source, legacy13MHz, no high-speed caps | Retain last-resort known storage path |
| y2_clock_limit_hz | Runtime13/25/50/100/200MHz cap controls | Developer diagnostics only; ordinary tester not asked to tune live root |
| y2.usb_dma=off | PIO path / original packet layout | Use if required by successful RC transfer evidence |
| y2.rescue=1 | Explicit RAM rescue entry | Retain; requires packaged boot-argument path, not typing at a missing boot menu |
| Y2_MSDC_LAB | Header defaults1; root-only tuning mutation | Compile0 for public RC, read-only diagnostics retained |
| BR2_PACKAGE_Y2_CODEC_EXPERIMENTS | Builds FDK/freeaptx/LDAC optional encoders | Complete codec target and clear distribution independently; gate until approved, record any legally forced omission |
| C3 disable / cpu-idle-policy, dormant prerequisites; separate suspend owner flag | Baseline02 once-per-boot qualified CPU0 C3 activation with static readback and unchanged entry guards | C3 is bounded hardware-proven on UART01; new automatic cold boot pending. Full suspend owner flag does not confer acceptance |
| NCM/host role/USB audio modules | Available code, role/VBUS gate | NCM optional if ECM suffices; host conditional on board proof |
| Audio-enabled vs audio-qualified JSON | S16 44.1/48 enabled, narrower qualified profile | Keep honest evidence separation; implement nativeHD only after exact AFE proof, then qualify |
| Private SOC/power overrides; radio enable/codec preference; update channel | Validated persistent policy/config inputs | Document defaults and migration; no silent unsafe override |
| Root OTA/dev trust | Writer exists, no public channel | Hide/disable unqualified public action; manual beta updates accepted |

All700 extracted current/historical config/setup declarations are retained in JSON, including separate first-boot/development configs. Historical declarations are not all production flags. [CPU options](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/cpu-options.c), [local timer](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/local-timer.c), [USB](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/usb.c), [storage](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/storage-modes.h). Boot flags generally require a rebuilt/repackaged verified BOOTIMG; a normal tester currently has no on-device bootloader menu for them. Provide a matching prebuilt safe pair, not instructions to edit flash offsets.

## 21. KNOWN FAILURE MODES

| Failure | Evidence / likelihood status | Required containment / release effect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Appears bricked after bad BOOTIMG | Known single-slot rescue dependency; owner manual recovery history | Release gate: independently available correct loader/SPFT recovery and exact pair |
| Root/data/SD corruption | No current clean-media corruption established; SDR104 CRC faults and unqualified write paths are real risks | Release gate: highest robust qualified modes with safe fallbacks, verified hashes/fsck/shutdown and no live raw writes |
| Battery drains while “off” | Screen-off is awake; actual Power Off behavior needs RC confirmation | A/B poweroff and required sleep closure; screen-off drain is a distinct mode, not sleep acceptance |
| Cannot charge / net discharge under use | Configured charger and positive historical voltage trend, no complete acceptance | Release gate: attended ordinary-source/combined-load test |
| White/blank screen or no UI | Historical shutdown handoff issue; v2 source correction, supervisor budget exhaustion possible | Polished-release correction; blocks release if installation/recovery unusable |
| Bluetooth cannot reconnect / sink silent | Short SBC pass; lifecycle unqualified; historical PCM-name bug fixed | Required targeted test; blocks release for repeated core playback failure |
| USB disappears / cannot copy music | UART01 repaired path passes three 1 MiB bidirectional checksum rounds; older Fix02 failure remains historical | Carry forward bounded integrity; wider host/reconnect/large-transfer endurance and Baseline02 regression remain |
| Settings/bonds lost after update | Preserving package exists; current full migration scenario pending | Blocks release if loss reproduced; required acceptance rehearsal before release |
| OTA cannot recover | Host-tested only, one rescue slot | E manual update limitation only with qualified preserving recovery; D if advertised updater unsafe |
| Unsafe overclock/voltage / uncontrolled heat | Stock-bin guarded OPPs pass bounded tests;1300 all-core guard reached | Blocks release on observed unsafe behavior; existing cap/thermal authority, no ceiling expansion |
| Hidden SSH exposure/default credential | No such production source default found; private image not public proof | Blocks release if found; final image authorization/listener audit mandatory |
| Diagnostics leaks private library/network information | State export intentionally private; raw collectors not universal redaction | Pre-release: dedicated shareable bundle; do not upload backups by default |
| Wrong Y2 revision/layout | Only one board family qualified; mismatch risk unknown | Compatibility release gate: reject unexpected identity, no guessed writes |
| Public image contains owner-only blobs/uncleared codec binaries | Confirmed private packaging assumptions | Distribution release gate; endpoint-off does not remove binaries |

## 22. RELEASE COMPLETION GATES

The former seven safety/distribution gates are retained: fresh-owner installation/recovery/keys/compatibility; lawful package composition/source/notices; storage integrity; USB transfer; charging/shutdown reserve; integrated endurance; and preserving-update/package rehearsal. They are necessary but insufficient for the intended beta. Add feature gates for native meaningful24-bit and88.2/96 where supported, the viable Classic codec set/policy/coexistence, affected regression of qualified C2/C3 and new automatic C3 startup, full same-boot system sleep/wake/restoration, admitted high OPPs and fastest robust storage. Experimental labels allow bounded compatibility feedback, never corruption or unsafe operation.

The [current plan](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Frelease%2FY2-COMMUNITY-BETA-PLAN.md) records the remaining Baseline02 and release boundaries; earlier three-pass schedules are historical. C3 support is physically proved on UART01; other hardware-conditional items remain unresolved release decisions until exact proof establishes support or a genuine limit. Missing instruments, missing peers, insufficient time or reverse-engineering difficulty are not unsupported-hardware evidence. Any permitted distribution omission remains a feature target with an explicit public exception and owner acceptance; it is not reclassified post-beta.

## 23. ACCEPTABLE BETA LIMITATIONS

Manual preserving beta1→beta2 update can replace fully automatic OTA if verified update/recovery remains understandable. Estimated SOC may remain visibly labeled while truthful charging/warnings/shutdown work; calibrated current/temperature depend on actual board support. Narrow named peer/card/revision compatibility, bounded experimental labeling, software-resampler fallback for an unsupported source rate, SFTP rather than MTP, and best-effort cold-power log retention can be documented.

S16-only, SBC-only, permanently disabled deep sleep, or an arbitrary HS50 cap are not accepted solely because they are simpler. Lower audio/codec/storage modes and screen-off runtime idle remain safety fallbacks. A highest robust lower storage mode becomes the actual product ceiling only after correct supported-mode investigation and integrity evidence, not by deadline preference.

## 24. GENUINELY POST-BETA / UNSUPPORTED

Nonessential current extensions: writable engineering lab UI, CPU rasterizer fallback, network/video/DRM playback, advanced DAC impedance/DSD features beyond proven board wiring, AP/P2P/browser modes, NCM if ECM meets performance/reliability, extra Favorites/search/playlist screens outside the current Productv2 direction, automatic BOOTIMG/A-B OTA, and broad new security architecture. These are scope choices, not declarations of hardware incapability. Existing EQ processing/product exposure is now feature-completion work.

Do not pursue SD HS400 (not an SD mode). eMMC HS400 has no established supported MT6582 path and is outside the intended ceiling set. LE Audio has no supported controller ISO path; Adaptive/Lossless have no supported encoder/integration path in this stack. Do not turn those into fake release requirements. FM/speaker absence in current software and unproven host VBUS/ID do not establish physical impossibility: FM/speaker remain nonessential/unproven, host is explicitly conditional. Only evidence can upgrade a conditional feature to unsupported.

## 25. PRESERVED FOUNDATIONS AND COMPLETION GROUPS

Carry forward stock ROM/preloader/LK/protected partitions, memory/reservations, bounded timer/SMP/DVFS voltage/thermal results, awake Lima, existing audio/DSP decode, one reconnect owner, checksum fix, SQLite safety, platform boundary, cryptographic verification and preserving-image guards. Modify their interfaces only where required for explicit feature completion; retain focused regression and exact provenance. Do not freeze unfinished product features merely because their fallback works.

Every row has one product target and one or more completion groups; the following searchable index includes existing capabilities that must be carried forward. B does not mean every feature is physically untested: cited bounded passes remain valid and need only affected/integrated regression. A and C may overlap because implementation is conditional on proof; D is independent of technical maturity and also covers software/package/API release checks. `completion_status` distinguishes mature carry-forward from pending work. A B assignment is not a claim every feature needs a fresh isolated device experiment.

## 1. EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

Release definition: a polished, feature-complete community beta contains all intended major product capabilities. It may retain bugs, bounded experimental labels and documented peer/card compatibility limits. Major capabilities may be omitted only for demonstrated hardware limits, unresolved legal/distribution constraints, or genuinely nonessential experiments. A working lower mode, difficulty, reverse engineering or missing qualification does not make a product requirement optional. Safety remains mandatory: an experimental label never licenses corruption, unsafe power behavior or unusable recovery.

The existing functioning Linux music player and all233 feature rows /48 kernel patches remain the factual inventory. The release target includes high-quality wired audio and meaningful24-bit output, native88.2/96k where the exact AFE path permits it; viable Classic SBC/XQ/AAC/aptX/HD/LDAC/ABR; C2 and hardware-conditional C3; same-boot deep sleep with Power/RTC wake; all admitted598–1300MHz OPPs; fastest robust storage modes; reliable high-performance USB; battery/charging; polished Productv2; preserving update/recovery.

91 rows change from former C/D limitation/post-beta priorities to required or hardware-conditional scope. All233 receive independent product-target, implementation, evidence, distribution and completion-group fields. Counts:146 CORE_RELEASE_FEATURE,59 FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET,5 CONDITIONAL_ON_HARDWARE,7 ACCEPTABLE_BETA_LIMITATION,1 UNSUPPORTED_HARDWARE and15 EXPERIMENTAL_POST_BETA. Native24-bit and SDR104 retain hardware conditions within FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET, so there are7 hardware-conditional rows overall. These are scope counts, not claims of readiness. Historical A–E/beta labels remain only in JSON `legacy_release_classification`, with exact promoted IDs and a counting rule.

Current verdict: FEATURE COMPLETION IN PROGRESS. Native high-resolution wired output remains unresolved; optional Classic endpoints are now enabled in an explicit private integration profile. Full resume and C3 still require physical closure. The previous seven safety/distribution gates remain necessary but do not alone define completion. Plan: three batched engineering passes → one integrated RC → physical qualification/endurance → release. A seven-day target is aggressive and conditional; unresolved required capabilities delay beta rather than silently shrinking its feature set.

Candidate4 is now sealed/software-validated (304 tests in receipt), unflashed/unqualified. Candidate3 remains the latest reported installed/SD-observed pair; Fix02 remains the latest broad hardware qualification. This reclassification ran no implementation, build, flash, physical test or push.
