### Evidence and maturity rules

Product target is independent of implementation and evidence. Every row retains its original implementation/evidence unless a newer receipt justifies a correction (F233: UNTESTED→SOFTWARE_VALIDATED from candidate4 seal). NOT_IMPLEMENTED means absent end-to-end capability even when helpers exist. The schema preserves STUB_OR_PLACEHOLDER and UNIT_TESTED for historical compatibility; neither is used to inflate evidence. PHYSICALLY_QUALIFIED means the cited bounded older pass, not whole-current-candidate acceptance; all current-candidate qualification flags remain false. No ENDURANCE_QUALIFIED result is invented.

Product axes: CORE_RELEASE_FEATURE, FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET, CONDITIONAL_ON_HARDWARE, ACCEPTABLE_BETA_LIMITATION, UNSUPPORTED_HARDWARE, EXPERIMENTAL_POST_BETA. `release_required` is REQUIRED, REQUIRED_IF_HARDWARE_SUPPORTED or NOT_REQUIRED; `hardware_conditional` and `public_distribution_state` remain independent. The additional `release_requirement` distinguishes polished UX from feature and conditional completion. A technically viable codec can be FEATURE_COMPLETE_TARGET with DISTRIBUTION_BLOCKED_OR_REVIEW_REQUIRED. NOT_SEPARATELY_ASSESSED distribution status is not clearance.

Completion groups A–G now mean implementation, physical qualification, hardware condition, release/distribution, acceptable limitation, genuine experiment, and unsupported respectively; they are not the old A–E priorities. Groups can overlap. Existing bounded passes carry forward with affected/integrated regression; a B assignment is not233 new isolated hardware experiments. Historic classifications are retained in JSON solely for comparison.

## 5. CPU / POWER

### Current CPU idle evidence — 2026-10-03

Installed baseline Linux131ee46/Reborn7f9df39, same bootcee326c1, now physically closes C1 and C2: four-core WFI residency/entries/rejections pass; natural parking reaches CPU0/owner0xe; SLIDLE adds7182 entries and50.763741 seconds in60.404076 seconds (84%), with clock restore failures0 and fsynced eMMC/inserted-SD integrity. All five guarded OPPs and three physical hotplug cycles pass. Real wired playback adds352256 decoded frames, errors0, real PlaybackNormal lease and all-core demand wake. These results supersede the historical C2 gap below for the installed baseline; the coherent new candidate still requires qualification. C3 remains SOFTWARE_READY_NEEDS_NEW_FLASH, default off until one backstopped entry and 20 checked cycles. No battery-life or full-system-suspend pass is claimed. [Physical record](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-IDLE-COMPLETION-PHYSICAL.md), [implementation and harness](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-IDLE-COMPLETION.md).

| OPP | Source voltage | Strongest physical evidence | Beta interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 598 MHz | 1.15 V | Fix02 clock/readback/integrity and idle | Carry forward |
| 747.5 MHz | 1.15 V | Fix02 QoS floors, readback and interaction | Carry forward |
| 1040 MHz | 1.15 V | Fix02 four-worker integrity16647 iterations, no errors | Carry forward |
| 1196 MHz | 1.20 V | Fix02 integrity18604 iterations, peak71.5°C | Required admitted OPP; mixed-load/endurance/charging comfort remains |
| 1300 MHz | 1.25 V | Two workers5247 iterations/no errors; four workers reached75°C test guard in16.8s | Not evidence of a safe sustained worst-case thermal envelope |

Bin and PWRAP admission are real:7-bit arbiter reads `0x7f`, selectors `0x48/0x50/0x58`; unknown prerequisites gate higher modes. Fix03 changes `admission_error=1` (successful QoS change return) to0; this is a label correction, not a newly safe voltage. Thermal cooling overrode live QoS down to598 MHz. The sampled7796 frequency/voltage pairs establish bounded ordering observations, not an electrical waveform capture. [Fix02 report](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fvalidation%2FY2-CPU-FINAL-FIX02-PHYSICAL-QUALIFICATION.md), [DVFS source](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/cpu-dvfs.c).

Eight bounded workload classes exist: Idle, PlaybackNormal, PlaybackHeavy, Interactive, ArtworkDecode, LibraryScan, NetworkTransfer, Maintenance. Application producers request semantic leases; the platform owns frequency policy. Reborn's partial-write lease defect was repaired before Fix02. Existing expiry and thermal precedence should not change before beta.

GPT6 supplies the13-MHz basis; architectural clocksource and per-CPU PPI29 clockevents work with CNTFRQ13MHz on every CPU. GPT4 is sole broadcast owner. Fix02 highres/nohz and ~1.09ms median requested1ms sleeps pass. C1 WFI works. C2 SLIDLE failed because inherited TOPCKGEN+4 was0 instead of expected0x0f; Fix03 admits both source-backed baselines and preserves checked0x8f programming/restore. It has no subsequent full physical pass. C3 stays default off and unqualified; runtime CIRQ/deadline handoff is distinct from system suspend.

Automatic parking worked3→2→1 at5-second pacing; display wake restored all CPUs in0.63s. Fix03 prevents the wake's own burst from doubling hold time. Screen-off idle improved to2.71% of four-core capacity /171 IRQ/s in a330-second sample with USB, charging and radios; it is not measured battery endurance. Screen-off runtime idle remains required for playback and fallback, but is not the full-sleep product. Close C2; investigate and qualify C3 if viable; full same-boot sleep/wake is a core release requirement.

CPU/PMIC die sensors are not battery sensors. DT retains110°C passive/120°C critical CPU and150°C PMIC critical trips. Their natural intervention is not physically exercised at the high ceiling. The previous owner's accepted thresholds are not changed by this plan. Test realistic charging+playback/scan/radio load under the existing attended guard, and use an existing lower ceiling if needed; do not pursue higher voltages or thermal limits.

### CPU idle completion ledger (F028–F030)

C1 is mature. C2 is implemented/enabled: Fix02 saw zero entries because bus DCM read0 instead of0x0f; Fix03 accepts both with exact restore. Its release gap is actual entry/residency, timer continuity and wake under normal parking. C3 is implemented/disabled and CONDITIONAL_ON_HARDWARE, with substantial existing code, not a blank implementation project.````

| C3 prerequisite | Current source / evidence | Required closure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Topology/OPP | spm.c requires CPU0 alone, CPUs1–3 physically off, frequency≤747.5MHz; existing parking works | Observe normal prerequisite admission; do not force unsafe hotplug or confuse C3 entry-frequency cap with active1300MHz removal |
| Runtime PCM | Separate stock deep-idle PCM, infra/DDRPHY retained; normal PCM restored on abort/return | Validate runtime program behavior on board; system-suspend PCM proof is not equivalent |
| CPU context | cpu_suspend, Linux MMU/CP15/VFP/GIC ownership, BIU/L2 register save/restore, boot resume vector | Same-boot register/timer/control integrity after actual dormant return and abort |
| CIRQ | Begin clones/masks, end replays; readiness is required | Actual pending interrupt replay, no lost/duplicated wake |
| GPT4 deadline | Sole broadcast clockevent; C3 TIMER_STOP/OFF, monotonic next timer must exceed2ms | Prove earliest-deadline handoff and interrupt wake, near/past deadline refusal, no time jump or stalled per-CPU timers |
| Domains/clocks | Both SPM power status registers, PERI/INFRA masks, checked bus baseline; preflight lists every unmet condition | Resolve actual blockers without deleting safety predicates; verify exact clock restore |
| Entry/wake | No retained C3 entry; failures/aborts latch broken state and fall back to C2/WFI | Entry/resume evidence, repeated idle/wake and measured practical power benefit |

Sources: [SPM](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm.c), [idle](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/idle.c), [runtime policy](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm-idle-policy.h), [runtime PCM](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/spm-idle-pcm.h), [resume vector](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/resume-vector.S), [local timer](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/local-timer.c), [CIRQ](https://github.com/schulzcode/y2linux/blob/HEAD/kernel/platform/cirq.c). C2+parking could offer a comparable practical idle result only if measured drain/latency demonstrates it; current CPU percentages do not. Such an explicit product tradeoff requires evidence and owner decision, not automatic C3 deferral. Full system sleep remains a separate core feature regardless.

These are Reborn (Y2Linux)'s own instructions, shown here word for word.

Published by the [schulzcode/y2linux](https://innioasis.app/firmware.html?model=Y2&software=reborn-y2linux-y2) project [[1]](https://innioasis.app/firmware_info.html?repo=schulzcode%2Fy2linux&software=reborn-y2linux-y2)[[3]](https://github.com/y1-community/Innioasis-Updater/pulls?q=is%3Apr%20author%3Aschulzcode)
